&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Simple types ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- Message type definitions -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="MessageType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Message type defines the type of reporting&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- MessageTypeIndic – 101 to 103 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="MessageTypeIndic_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">The MessageTypeIndic defines the type of message sent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR101">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message contains new information&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR102">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message contains corrections for previously sent information&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR103">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message advises there is no data to report&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--GLOBE #2 Set of Enumerations – 201 to 205 – ID Type – Rules-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="IDTypeRules_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">ID Type – Rules&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR201">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>QIIR applicable to Low-Taxed Constituent Entities located in other jurisdictions only&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR202">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>QIIR applicable to both Low-Taxed Constituent Entities located in other jurisdictions and in the jurisdiction of the Parent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR203">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>QUTPR&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR204">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>QDMTT&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR205">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Not applicable&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--GLOBE #3 Set of Enumerations – 301 to 317 – ID Type – GloBEStatus-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="IDTypeGloBEStatus_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">ID Type – GloBEStatus&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR301">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Constituent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR302">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Flow-Through Entity – Tax Transparent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR303">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Flow-Through Entity – Reverse Hybrid&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR304">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Hybrid Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR305">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Permanent Establishment&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR306">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Main Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR307">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Minority-Owned Parent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR308">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Minority-Owned Subsidiary&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR309">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Minority-Owned Constituent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR310">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Investment Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR311">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Insurance Investment Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR312">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Securitisation Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR313">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>JV&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR314">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>JV Subsidiary&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR315">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Non-Material Constituent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR316">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Excluded Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR317">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Parent Entity required to apply a QIIR under Article 10.3.5&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR318">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Non-group Member&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--GLOBE #4 Set of Enumerations – 401 to 405 – FilingInfo – FilingCE – Role-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="FilingCERole_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">FilingInfo – FilingCE – Role&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR401">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Ultimate Parent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR402">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Designated Filing Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR403">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Designated Local Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR404">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Constituent Entity&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR405">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Other&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--GLOBE #5 Set of Enumerations – 501 to 504 – FilingInfo – AccountingInfo – CFsofUPE-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="FilingCECofUPE_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">FilingInfo – AccountingInfo – CFsofUPE&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR501">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Subparagraph a&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;!--GLOBE #27 Set of Enumerations – 2701 to 2720 – 3.2.1.2 Computation of Adjusted Covered Taxes-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="FinalAdjustedTax_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">3.2.1.2 Computation of Adjusted Covered Taxes&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2701">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Covered Tax accrued as an expense in the profit before taxation in the financial accounts – Article 4.1.2 (a)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2702">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>GloBE Loss Deferred Tax Asset established under Article 4.5.1 or used – Article 4.1.2 (b) combined with Article 4.5.3&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2703">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Covered Taxes for uncertain tax position recorded as a reduction to Covered Taxes in prior year – Article 4.1.2 (c)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2704">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Qualified Refundable Tax Credit or Marketable Transferable Tax Credits recorded as a reduction to current tax expense – Article 4.1.2 (d)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2705">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Qualified Flow-through Tax Benefits of Qualified Ownership Interests – Article 3.2.1 (c)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2706">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Current tax expense on income excluded from GloBE Income or Loss – Article 4.1.3 (a)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2707">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Non-Qualified Refundable Tax Credit, Non-Marketable Transferable Tax Credit or Other Tax Credits not recorded as a reduction to current tax expense – Article 4.1.3 (b)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2708">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Covered Taxes refunded or credited (except for any Qualified Refundable Tax Credit, or Marketable Transferable Tax Credits) not treated as an adjustment to current tax expense – Article 4.1.3 (c)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2709">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Current tax expense related to uncertain tax position– Article 4.1.3 (d)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2710">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Current tax expense not expected to be paid within three years – Article 4.1.3 (e)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2711">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Post-filing adjustments – Article 4.6.1.&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2712">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Covered Taxes relating to Net Asset Gain or Net Asset Loss – Article 3.2.6&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2713">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Reduction of Covered Taxes of the UPE that is a Flow-through Entity – Article 7.1&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2714">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Covered Taxes for GloBE Income of the UPE that is reduced under a Deductible Dividend Regime – Article 7.2.2&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2715">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Deemed Distribution Tax – Article 7.3&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2716">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Taxable Distribution Method election – Article 7.6 (b)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2717">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Total Deferred Tax Adjustment Amount – Article 4.4.1(b)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2718">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Increase or decrease in Covered Taxes recorded in equity or Other Comprehensive Income relating to amounts included in GloBE Income or Loss that will be subject to tax under local tax rules – Article 4.1.1 (c)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2719">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Excess Negative Tax Expense Carry Forward generated – Article 4.1.5 and 5.2.1&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="GIR2720">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Decrease in Covered Taxes (but not below zero) by the remaining balance of the Excess Negative Tax Expense Carry-forward – Article 4.1.5 and 5.2.1&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!--++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Complex types +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- Message specification: Data identifying and describing the message as a whole -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--Message specification: Data identifying and describing the message as a whole-->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="MessageSpec_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Information in the message header identifies the Tax Administration that is sending the message. It specifies when the message was created, what period (normally a year) the report is for, and the nature of the report (original, corrected, supplemental, etc.).

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="SendingEntityIN" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransmittingCountry" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;!-- modified for GLOBE -->

&lt;xsd:element name="ReceivingCountry" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageType" type="globe:MessageType_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Warning" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Contact" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageRefId" type="stf:StringMin1Max170_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageTypeIndic" type="globe:MessageTypeIndic_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="ReportingPeriod" type="xsd:date"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Timestamp" type="xsd:dateTime"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- TIN -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="TIN_Type">

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="issuedBy" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="optional"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="unknown" type="xsd:boolean" use="optional"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="TypeOfTIN" type="globe:TIN_EnumType"/>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- GlobeOECD File Message structure -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="GLOBEBody_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="FilingInfo">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="globe:FilingInfo">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:complexContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="GeneralSection" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="globe:GeneralSectionType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:complexContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="Summary" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="globe:SummaryType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:complexContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurisdictionSection" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="globe:JurisdictionSectionType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:complexContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="UTPRAttribution" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="globe:UTPRAttributionType">

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ Schema element ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- GLOBE Message structure -->

&lt;xsd:element name="GLOBE_OECD">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:element name="Art10.3.5" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ID" type="globe:ID_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="OwnershipChange" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Emumeration #8&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdditionalDataPoint" type="globe:AdditionalDataPointType" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexType name="JurisdictionSectionType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="RecJurCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Jurisdiction" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurWithTaxingRights" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurisdictionName" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Subgroup" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="TIN" type="globe:TIN_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TypeofSubGroup" type="globe:TypeofSubGroup_EnumType" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #11&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="ReportDifference" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ETRDifference" type="globe:percentage" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdjCoveredTaxDifference" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="AggCurrentTaxExpense" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="QRTCExpense" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="OtherTaxCredits" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="DeferTaxExpense" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="NetGLoBEDifference" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="SBIEDifference" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AddCurrentTuTDifference" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TuTDifference" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="ElectionsDifference" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="QRTCIncome" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="ExcessNegTaxCarryForw" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransitionDifference" type="xsd:boolean" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="LocalCurrency" type="iso:currCode_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="GLoBETax" type="globe:GLOBETax"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="LowTaxJurisdiction" type="globe:LowTaxJurisdictionType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdditionalDataPoint" type="globe:AdditionalDataPointType" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexType name="UTPRAttributionType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="RecJurCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Attribution" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ResCountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="UTPRTopUpTaxCarryForward" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Employees" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TangibleAssetValue" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="UTPRPercentage" type="globe:percentage">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>%&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="UTPRTopUpTaxAttributed" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AddCashTaxExpense" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="UTPRTopUpTaxCarriedForward" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdditionalDataPoint" type="globe:AdditionalDataPointType" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexType name="SummaryType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="RecJurCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Jurisdiction">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurisdictionName" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Subgroup" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="TIN" type="globe:TIN_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TypeofSubGroup" type="globe:TypeofSubGroup_EnumType" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #11&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurWithTaxingRights" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="JurisdictionName" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="DiffDomesticTut" type="globe:GlobeTuT_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="SafeHarbour" type="globe:SafeHarbour_EnumType" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #12&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="ETRRange" type="globe:ETRRange_EnumType" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #13&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="SBIE" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="NotApplicable" type="xsd:boolean"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NoTut" type="xsd:boolean"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="QDMTTut" type="globe:QDMTTuT_EnumType" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #14&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="GLoBETut" type="globe:GlobeTuT_EnumType" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Enumeration #15&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdditionalDataPoint" type="globe:AdditionalDataPointType" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:complexType name="AdditionalDataPointType">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="Description" type="stf:StringMin1Max170_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="xsd:integer" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Percentage" type="globe:percentage" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Text" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Boolean" type="xsd:boolean" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:schema>

## ANNEX XVIII — Computerised format referred to in Article 2(5)

The computerised format for the mandatory automatic exchange of information pursuant to Article 8ad(6) of Directive 2011/16/EU complies with the following tree structure and contains the following elements and attributes ( 10 ):

**CARF XML**

&lt;?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>

&lt;xsd:schema xmlns:carf="urn:oecd:ties:carf:v1" xmlns:xsd="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema" xmlns:stf="urn:oecd:ties:carfstf:v5" xmlns:iso="urn:oecd:ties:isocarftypes:v1" targetNamespace="urn:oecd:ties:carf:v1" elementFormDefault="qualified" attributeFormDefault="unqualified" version="1.3">

&lt;xsd:import namespace="urn:oecd:ties:isocarftypes:v1" schemaLocation="isocarftypes_v1.1.xsd"/>

&lt;xsd:import namespace="urn:oecd:ties:carfstf:v5" schemaLocation="oecdcarftypes_v5.0.xsd"/>

&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Simple types ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- Message type definitions -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="MessageType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Message type defines the type of reporting &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--Transfer Type - 401 to 404-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="ExchangeType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Transfer Type&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF401">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Staking&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF402">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Crypto Loan&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF403">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Wrapping&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF404">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Collateral&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--Transfer Type - 501 to 507-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="TransferType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Transfer Type&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF501">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Airdrop&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF502">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Staking income&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF503">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Mining income&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF504">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Crypto loan&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF505">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Transfer from another RCASP&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF506">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Sale of goods or services&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF507">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Collateral&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF508">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Other&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF509">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Unknown (to be selected as the default value where the Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider has no knowledge on the Transfer Type)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Account Number Type - 601 to 604-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="TransferOutType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Account Number Type&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF601">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Transfer to another RCASP&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF602">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Crypto Loan&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF603">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Purchase of goods or services, to be used in respect of transactions other than those already reported as Reportable Retail Payment Transactions&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF604">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Collateral&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF605">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Other&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF606">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Unknown (to be selected as the default value where the Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider has no knowledge on the Transfer Type)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- MessageTypeIndic - 701 to 703 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="CarfMessageTypeIndic_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">The MessageTypeIndic defines the type of message sent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF701">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message contains new information&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF702">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message contains corrections/deletions for previously sent information. When the MesseageTypeIndic is CARF702, the DocTypeIndic can contain either Corrections (OECD2) or Deletions (OECD3) or both, but new data (OECD1) cannot be contained. Note that OECD0 can be included for RCASP's DocTypeIndic.&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF703">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>The message advises there is no data to report&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Controlling Person Type - 801 to 813 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="CarfCtrlgPersonType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Controlling Person Type&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF801">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal person - ownership&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF802">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal person - other means&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF803">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal person - senior managing official&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF804">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - trust - settlor&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF805">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - trust - trustee&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF806">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - trust - protector&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF807">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - trust - beneficiary&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF808">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - trust - other&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF809">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - other - settlor-equivalent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF810">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - other - trustee-equivalent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF811">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - other - protector-equivalent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF812">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - other - beneficiary-equivalent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF813">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>CP of legal arrangement - other - other-equivalent&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--CARF Nexus Type - 901 to 907 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="Nexus_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF901">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Tax Residence&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF902">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Incorporation&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF903">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Management&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF904">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Place of Business&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF905">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Branch&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF906">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Authorisation&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF907">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Remote Services&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--CARF Alt Valuation Type - 1001 to 1004 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="AltValuation_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF1001">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Book value&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF1002">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Third-party value&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF1003">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Recent RCASP valuation&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="CARF1004">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Reasonable estimate by RCASP&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="TwoDigFract_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Data type for any kind of numeric data with two decimal fraction digits, especially monetary amounts.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:decimal">

&lt;xsd:fractionDigits value="2"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="SixDigFract_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Data type for any kind of numeric data with six decimal fraction digits, especially monetary amounts.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:decimal">

&lt;xsd:fractionDigits value="6"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Complex types +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- Message specification: Data identifying and describing the message as a whole -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="RCASP_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="RCASP_ID">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:choice>

&lt;xsd:element name="Individual" type="carf:PersonParty_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Entity" type="carf:OrganisationParty_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="Nexus" type="carf:Nexus_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="OtherNexus" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="Nexus" type="carf:Nexus_EnumType" use="required"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="ResCountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="required"/>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="CryptoUsers_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="UserID">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:choice>

&lt;xsd:element name="Entity" type="carf:OrganisationParty_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Individual" type="carf:PersonParty_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="ControllingPerson" type="carf:ControllingPerson_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="RelevantTransactions" type="carf:RelevantTransactions_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocSpec" type="stf:DocSpec_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="RelevantTransactions_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoAsset" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:choice>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoToCryptoIn" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ExchangeType" type="carf:ExchangeType_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoToCryptoOut" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ExchangeType" type="carf:ExchangeType_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoFiatIn" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ExchangeType" type="carf:ExchangeType_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoFiatOut" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ExchangeType" type="carf:ExchangeType_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoTransferIn" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransferType" type="carf:TransferType_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AltValuation" type="carf:AltValuation_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoTransferOut" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransferType" type="carf:TransferOutType_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AltValuation" type="carf:AltValuation_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransferWallet" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AltValuation" type="carf:AltValuation_EnumType" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="RRPT" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Reportable Retail Payment Transactions (Relevant Crypto Assets)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofTransactions" type="xsd:integer"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Amount" type="carf:MonAmnt_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="NumberofUnits" type="xsd:decimal"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="ControllingPerson_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="Individual" type="carf:PersonParty_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CtrlgPersonType" type="carf:CarfCtrlgPersonType_EnumType" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="Identity_Type">

&lt;xsd:choice>

&lt;xsd:element name="Individual" type="carf:PersonParty_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Entity" type="carf:OrganisationParty_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!--Message specification: Data identifying and describing the message as a whole-->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="MessageSpec_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Information in the message header identifies the Tax Administration that is sending the message. It specifies when the message was created, what period (normally a year) the report is for, and the nature of the report (original, corrected, supplemental, etc).&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="SendingEntityIN" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TransmittingCountry" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="ReceivingCountry" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;!-- modified for CARF -->

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageType" type="carf:MessageType_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Warning" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Contact" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageRefId" type="stf:StringMin1Max170_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageTypeIndic" type="carf:CarfMessageTypeIndic_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="ReportingPeriod" type="xsd:date"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Timestamp" type="xsd:dateTime"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- The Name of a Party, given in fixed Form-->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="NamePerson_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">The user must spread the data about the name of a party over up to six elements. The container element for this will be 'NameFix'. &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="PrecedingTitle" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Title" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="FirstName">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="xnlNameType" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Defines the name type of FirstName. Example: Given Name, Forename, Christian Name, Father's Name, etc. In some countries, FirstName could be a Family Name or a SurName. Use this attribute to define the type for this name.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="MiddleName" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="xnlNameType" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Defines the name type of Middle Name. Example: First name, middle name, maiden name, father's name, given name, etc.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="NamePrefix" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="xnlNameType" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Defines the type of name associated with the NamePrefix. For example the type of name is LastName and this prefix is the prefix for this last name.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="LastName">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="xnlNameType" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Defines the name type of LastName. Example: Father's name, Family name, Sur Name, Mother's Name, etc. In some countries, LastName could be the given name or first name.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="GenerationIdentifier" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Suffix" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="GeneralSuffix" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="nameType" type="stf:OECDNameType_EnumType" use="optional"/>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Collection of all Data describing a person as a Party -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="PersonParty_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ResCountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TIN" type="carf:TIN_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="IIN" type="carf:IndividualIN_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Entity Identification Number&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="Name" type="carf:NamePerson_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Address" type="carf:Address_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Nationality" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="BirthInfo">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>.&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="BirthDate" type="xsd:date"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="BirthPlace" type="carf:BirthPlace_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--General Type for Monetary Amounts -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="MonAmnt_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">This data type is to be used whenever monetary amounts are to be communicated. Such amounts shall be given in with full amounts and two decimals. The code for the currency in which the value is expressed has to be taken from the ISO codelist 4217 and added in attribute currCode.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="xsd:decimal">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="currCode" type="iso:currCode_Type" use="required"/>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Organisation Identification Number -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="OrganisationIN_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">This is the identification number/identification code for the Entity in question. As the identifier may be not strictly numeric, it is just defined as a string of characters. Attribute 'issuedBy' is required to designate the issuer of the identifier. Attribute 'INType' defines the type of identification number. &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="issuedBy" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="optional">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Country code of issuing country, indicating country of Residence (to taxes and other)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="INType" use="optional">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Identification Number Type&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:simpleType>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="LEI"/>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="EIN"/>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BRN"/>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="Other"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Individual Identification Number -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="IndividualIN_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">This is the identification number/identification code for the Entity in question. As the identifier may be not strictly numeric, it is just defined as a string of characters. Attribute 'issuedBy' is required to designate the issuer of the identifier. Attribute 'INType' defines the type of identification number. &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="issuedBy" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="optional">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Country code of issuing country, indicating country of Residence (to taxes and other)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Collection of all Data describing an organisation as party-->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="OrganisationParty_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="ResCountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="TIN" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="carf:TIN_Type">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="1"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="issuedBy" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="optional"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="unknown" type="xsd:boolean" use="optional"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="IN" type="carf:OrganisationIN_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Entity Identification Number&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="IIN" type="carf:IndividualIN_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Entity Identification Number&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="Name" type="carf:NameOrganisation_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="Address" type="carf:Address_Type" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Address Fix -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="AddressFix_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="Street" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="BuildingIdentifier" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="SuiteIdentifier" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="FloorIdentifier" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="DistrictName" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="POB" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="PostCode" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="City" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CountrySubentity" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- The Address of a Party, given in fixed or free Form, possibly in both Forms -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="Address_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="CountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="AddressFix" type="carf:AddressFix_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="AdditionalAddressInfo" type="stf:StringMin1Max4000_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="legalAddressType" type="stf:OECDLegalAddressType_EnumType" use="optional"/>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Organisation name -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="NameOrganisation_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Name of organisation&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="nameType" type="stf:OECDNameType_EnumType" use="optional"/>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- TIN -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="TIN_Type">

&lt;xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;xsd:extension base="stf:StringMin0Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:attribute name="issuedBy" type="iso:CountryCode_Type" use="optional"/>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="unknown" type="xsd:boolean" use="optional"/>

&lt;/xsd:extension>

&lt;/xsd:simpleContent>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--The place of birth -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="BirthPlace_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>This element provides information about the place of birth. This element must be filled in at least with the city and the country of birth (either the current jurisdiction identified by 2-characters country code or a former jurisdiction identified by a name).&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="City" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CitySubentity" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CountryInfo">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:choice>

&lt;xsd:element name="CountryCode" type="iso:CountryCode_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="FormerCountryName" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type"/>

&lt;/xsd:choice>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- CARF Body Type - CARF Report -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="CARFBody_Type">

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="RCASP" type="carf:RCASP_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CryptoUsers" type="carf:CryptoUsers_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ Schema element ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- CarfOECD File Message structure -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- CARF Message structure -->

&lt;xsd:element name="CARF_OECD">

&lt;xsd:complexType>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="MessageSpec" type="carf:MessageSpec_Type"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="CARFBody" type="carf:CARFBody_Type" minOccurs="0" maxOccurs="unbounded"/>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:attribute name="version" type="stf:StringMin1Max10_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">CARF Version&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:attribute>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:schema>

**ISO CARF TYPES**

&lt;?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>

&lt;xsd:schema xmlns:iso="urn:oecd:ties:isocarftypes:v1" xmlns:xsd="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema" targetNamespace="urn:oecd:ties:isocarftypes:v1" elementFormDefault="qualified" attributeFormDefault="unqualified" version="1.1">

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="CountryCode_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">ISO-3166 Alpha 2 country codes&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AF">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>AFGHANISTAN&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AX">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ALAND ISLANDS&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AL">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ALBANIA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="DZ">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ALGERIA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AS">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>AMERICAN SAMOA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AD">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ANDORRA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AO">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ANGOLA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AI">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ANGUILLA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AQ">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ANTARCTICA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AG">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ANTIGUA AND BARBUDA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AR">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ARGENTINA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AM">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ARMENIA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AW">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>ARUBA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AU">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>AUSTRALIA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AT">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>AUSTRIA&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="AZ">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>AZERBAIJAN&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BS">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BAHAMAS&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BH">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BAHRAIN&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BD">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BANGLADESH&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BB">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BARBADOS&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BY">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BELARUS&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="BE">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>BELGIUM&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;!-- ISO 4217 alpha 3 Currency Code

The following disclaimer refers to all uses of the ISO currency code list in the CARF schema: For practical reasons, the list is based on the ISO 4217 Alpha 3 currency list which is currently used by banks and other financial institutions, and hence by tax administrations. The use of this list does not imply the expression by the OECD of any opinion whatsoever concerning the legal status of the territories listed. Its content is without prejudice to the status of or sovereignty over any territory, to the delimitation of international frontiers and boundaries and to the name of any territory, city or area.

-->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="currCode_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">

The appropriate currency code from the ISO 4217 three-byte alpha version for the currency in which a monetary amount is expressed.

**OECD CARF TYPES**

&lt;?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>

&lt;xsd:schema xmlns:stf="urn:oecd:ties:carfstf:v5" xmlns:xsd="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema" targetNamespace="urn:oecd:ties:carfstf:v5" elementFormDefault="qualified" attributeFormDefault="unqualified" version="5.0">

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++ String length types ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 0 and maximum length of 200 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin0Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 0 and maximum length of 200.&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="0"/>

&lt;xsd:maxLength value="200"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 10 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin1Max10_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 10&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="1"/>

&lt;xsd:maxLength value="10"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 170 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin1Max170_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 170&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="1"/>

&lt;xsd:maxLength value="170"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 200 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 200&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="1"/>

&lt;xsd:maxLength value="200"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 400 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin1Max400_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 400&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 4000 -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="StringMin1Max4000_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Defines a string with minimum length 1 and maximum length of 4000&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:minLength value="1"/>

&lt;xsd:maxLength value="4000"/>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--+++++++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Simple types ++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- Document type indicators types -->

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">This element specifies the type of data being submitted.&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD2">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD3">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD10">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD11">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD12">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Corrected Test Data&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD13">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>Deletion of Test Data&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Kind of Name -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="OECDNameType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">

It is possible for stf documents to contain several names for the same party. This is a qualifier to indicate the type of a particular name. Such types include nicknames ('nick'), names under which a party does business ('dba' a short name for the entity, or a name that is used for public acquaintance instead of the official business name) etc.

&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:string">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD201">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>SMFAliasOrOther&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD202">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>indiv (individual)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD203">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>alias (alias)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD204">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>nick (nickname)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD205">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>aka (also known as)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD206">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>dba (doing business as)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD207">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>legal (legal name)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD208">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>atbirth (name at birth)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Type of the address considered from a legal point of view -->

&lt;xsd:simpleType name="OECDLegalAddressType_EnumType">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">This is a datatype for an attribute to an address. It serves to indicate the legal character of that address (residential, business etc.)&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:restriction base="xsd:token">

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD301">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>residentialOrBusiness&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD302">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>residential&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD303">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>business&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD304">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>registeredOffice&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;xsd:enumeration value="OECD305">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation>unspecified&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:enumeration>

&lt;/xsd:restriction>

&lt;/xsd:simpleType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!--++++++++++++++++++ Reusable Complex types +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++ -->

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;!-- Document specification: Data identifying and describing the document -->

&lt;xsd:complexType name="DocSpec_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Document specification: Data identifying and describing the document, where 'document' here means the part of a message that is to transmit the data about a single block of carf information. &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:sequence>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocTypeIndic" type="stf:OECDDocTypeIndic_EnumType"/>

&lt;xsd:element name="DocRefId" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Sender's unique identifier of this document &lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CorrMessageRefId" type="stf:StringMin1Max170_Type" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Reference id of the message of the document referred to if this is a correction&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;xsd:element name="CorrDocRefId" type="stf:StringMin1Max200_Type" minOccurs="0">

&lt;xsd:annotation>

&lt;xsd:documentation xml:lang="en">Reference id of the document referred to if this is correction&lt;/xsd:documentation>

&lt;/xsd:annotation>

&lt;/xsd:element>

&lt;/xsd:sequence>

&lt;/xsd:complexType>

&lt;!-- -->

&lt;/xsd:schema>

## ANNEX XIX — Form referred to in Article 2g(1) and (2)

The form to be used for the communication of information on a Crypto-Asset Operator that is a Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider as defined in Section IV, subparagraph B(3), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU contains, in addition to the components listed in Section V, subparagraph F(2), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU, the following fields:

— Member State of single registration in accordance with Article 8ad(7) of Directive 2011/16/EU;

**The format of the individual identification number referred to in Article 2g(3)**

The individual identification number consists of a 10-digit format, structured as CCXXXXXXXX, where 'CC' represents the ISO Country Code of the Member State where the identification number is issued and 'XXXXXXXX' is a unique sequence of alphanumeric characters, generated and assigned to the Crypto-Asset Operator either by the Member State or by the Crypto Asset Operator register. The individual identification number is allocated by the Crypto-Asset Operator register when not assigned by the Member State.

**Retention period for certain information deleted or removed from the Crypto-Asset Operator register referred to in Article 2g (4)**

Where the registration of a Crypto-Asset Operator that is a Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider as defined in Section IV, subparagraph B(3), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU, is revoked in accordance with Section V, subparagraph F(7), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU, the Member State of single registration shall delete the Crypto-Asset Operator from the Crypto-Asset Operator register. The Crypto-Asset Operator register shall retain the deleted information for no longer than 12 months from the date of deletion.

**Change of the Member State of single registration in the Crypto-Asset Operator register referred to in Article 2g(5)**

If the change in the information pursuant to Section V, subparagraph F(3), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU involves the change of the Member State of single registration, the current Member State of single registration triggers a transfer of the information of the registered Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider to the new Member State of single registration. The new Member State of single registration becomes the Member State of single registration where the information under Directive 2011/16/EU is to be reported. This Member State shall be the new point of contact for the Reporting Crypto-Asset Service provider for updating any information in accordance with Section V, subparagraph F(3), of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU and this Regulation.

The individual identification number allocated to the Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider at its first single registration remains the same throughout the process.

This process shall not relieve the Reporting Crypto-Asset Service Provider from following domestic registration requirements in the new Member State of single registration.

( 1 ) Regulation (EU) 2016/679 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 27 April 2016 on the protection of natural persons with regard to the processing of personal data and on the free movement of such data, and repealing Directive 95/46/EC (General Data Protection Regulation) (OJ L 119, 4.5.2016, p. 1).

( 2 ) Regulation (EU) 2018/1725 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 October 2018 on the protection of natural persons with regard to the processing of personal data by the Union institutions, bodies, offices and agencies and on the free movement of such data, and repealing Regulation (EC) No 45/2001 and Decision No 1247/2002/EC (OJ L 295, 21.11.2018, p. 39).

( 3 ) However, only the fields actually filled in a given case need to appear in the form used in that case

( 4 ) However, only the fields actually filled in a given case need to appear in the form used in that case.

( 5 ) However, only the fields actually filled in a given case need to appear in the form used in that case.

( 6 ) However, only the fields actually filled in a given case need to appear in the form used in that case.

( 7 ) However, only the classes of elements actually available and applicable in a given case need to appear in the computerised format used in that case.

( 8 ) However, only the elements and attributes actually applicable in a given case further to the performance of the reporting and due diligence rules included in Annexes I and II to Directive 2011/16/EU need to appear in the computerised format used in that case.

( 9 ) However, only the elements and attributes actually applicable in a given case further to the performance of the due diligence and reporting rules included in Section II and Section III of Annex V of Council Directive 2011/16/EU, as amended by Council Directive (EU) 2021/514 need to appear in the computerised format used in that case.

( 10 ) However, only the elements and attributes actually applicable in a given case further to the performance of the due diligence and reporting rules included in Sections II and III of Annex VI to Directive 2011/16/EU need to appear in the computerised format used in that case.
